### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are non-zero vectors in which no two of them are perpendicular and no three of them are coplanar. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>s</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>s</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>s</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be a variable vector such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> are even natural numbers. If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>≤</mo><mn>20</mn></math>, then the number of values of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>60</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>75</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>120</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>120</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>y</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced close="}" open="" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>z</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> Integers (even natural numbers)
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn><mn>0</mn></math>
Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo> </mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>∀</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>∈</mo><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>
Now, adding a dummy variable <math><mi>ω</mi></math>, such that
<math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>k</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mfenced><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>w</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi>ys</mi><msup><mo>=</mo><mrow><mn>7</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msub><mi>C</mi><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></msup><msub><mi>C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math>
Hence, the number of values of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>120</mn></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If two points <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> lie on the curve <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, where <math><mi>O</mi></math> is the origin and <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> lie in the <math><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></msup></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></msup></math> quadrant respectively, then <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let the points <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mi></mi></math>  be <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> respectively where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For three non-zero vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>84</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>144</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>144</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>144</mn></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are perpendicular to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> respectively. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** 50
- **(C)** <math><mn>10</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** 10

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 10**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#10233;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#38;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> ...(i)

Given, |<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#10233;</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math> ....(ii)

Similarly, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>64</mn></math> ...(iii)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#38;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mi>&#160;</mi></math>....(iv)

On adding Eqs. (ii),(iii) and (iv), we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>200</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#10233;</mo><msup><mrow><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>...(v)[from Eqs. (i)]

Now, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><msup><mo>|</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mo>|</mo><msup><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#10233;</mo><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><msup><mo>|</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math> [from Eqs. (i) and (v)]

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#10233;</mo><mo>|</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><msup><mo>|</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> be two vectors such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> If angle between <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is equal to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>, then <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8869;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>28</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>28</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>33</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>as&#160;</mtext><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>90</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are a pair of mutually perpendicular vectors and if the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math><mi>θ</mi></math>, then the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>θ</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2500</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2499</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2500</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2499</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2500</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>25</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mn>25</mn></mfrac><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mn>50</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2499</mn><mn>2500</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8757;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The vertices <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> are <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7,6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> respectively. The measure of the interior angle <math><mi>A</mi></math> of the <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> lies in

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>90</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>45</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>120</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>150</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><msup><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/f9b803ba-d9ac-4fa3-9073-e2e87c2b4345.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.08681in;height:1.05347in" /></p><p>We have <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>11</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>,</mo><mn>75</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mrow><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> , then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>27</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>28</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>25</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>25</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mrow><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>5</mn></math>

now <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>-25</mn></math>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A unit vector in the <math><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math>-plane that makes an angle of <math><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> with the vector <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and an angle of <math><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> with the vector <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) None of these**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let the required unit vector be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>a</mi><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

Then, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#10233;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math>...(i)

Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> makes an angle of <math><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and an angle of <math><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> therefore

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mo>(</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>and</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#10233;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#10233;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>3</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#10233;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>13</mn><mn>14</mn></mfrac><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>14</mn></mfrac><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>&#729;</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are unit vectors making an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi></math> with each other, such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>α</mi></math> lies in the interval

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>25</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, then <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mn>29</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>.</mo></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is a non-zero vector perpendicular to both <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>, such that its component along <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>z</mi></math> axes are rational numbers, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** an integer
- **(B)** an irrational number
- **(C)** a rational number
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) an irrational number**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable equalrows="true" equalcolumns="true"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>&#955;</mi></mfenced></math></p><p>Hence, an irrational number</p>

</details>
