### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the angles between the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> respectively, then the angle which <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> makes with the plane containing <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

A normal vector perpendicular to the plane is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be two orthogonal unit vectors and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is a vector such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
Taking dot product with <math> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </math> , we get,
<math> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo> </mo></mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>As</mtext><mo> </mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow> </mfenced><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mn>..</mn><mfenced> <mtext>i</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
Taking dot product with <math> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </math> , we get, 
<math> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mn>..</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>ii</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
Taking dot product with <math> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </math> , we get, 
<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> <mtd> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mn>..</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>iii</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>−</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>​</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>
From <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced><mo> </mo><mo>&amp;</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi>ii</mi></mfenced></math>, we get, 
<math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>y</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are two non zero, non-collinear vectors satisfying <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>α</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>β</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>y</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>γ</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>y</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (where <math><mi>α</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>γ</mi></math> are three distinct numbers), then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>y</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are <math><mn>3</mn></math> non-coplanar vectors
Hence, <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>γ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>γ</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
We can say that <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> has roots <math><mi>α</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>γ</mi></math>
Since, quadratic has 3 roots so it becomes an identity
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>b</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>26</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>&#305;</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> where <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mi></mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3,6</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the number of non-zero vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>20</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>15</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>18</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> i.e. <math><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> i.e.<math><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> i.e.<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo></math> i.e.<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>!</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Total number of vectors <math><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn></math>

</details>

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Vertex <math><mi>A</mi></math> of the acute angled triangle <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is equidistant from its circumcentre <math><mi>O</mi></math> and orthocentre <math><mi>H</mi></math>, then the possible value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">∠</mi><mi>A</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>9</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, the position of circumcentre<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>O</mi><mo>)</mo></math> be origin, position vector of orthocentre<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi>H</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and position vectors of <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi></math> are <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> respectively
Centroid of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>G</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/62dda1cb-353f-4fde-9b8b-720f387f0e02.png" style="width:1.92014in;height:0.93333in" />
<math><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>H</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> (given)
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> equals to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>256</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>256</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>256</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p>Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>15</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>225</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>270</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>15</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>6</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2025</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>176</mn><mn>9</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1769</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2025</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>256</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2025</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>256</mn><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Consider <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn></math> non-zero vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></math>. If a vector <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> satisfies the relation <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>ii</mi></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mi>iii</mi></mfenced></math>,</p><p><math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#955;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>)</p>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider three vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi>R</mi></math>. If <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is maximum and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>, then the value of <math><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>For maximum <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> will be parallel to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> (as <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are three non-zero vectors such that the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>. If the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From the figure, it is clear that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> forms a triangle
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/a3394f9a-e883-43f9-9333-87aa72bade82.png" style="width:2.27361in;height:1.2in" />
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>18</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>18</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mn>4</mn></math>

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mn>4</mn></math>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>, then the maximum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is maximum, if <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is parallel to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
Hence, the maximum value <math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math><math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are two non-collinear vector such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The angles of a triangle whose two sides are represented by vectors <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> is perpendicular to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> other two angles are <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>j</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>29</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi></mi></math></p><p>Taking cross product with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on both the sides, we get,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"><mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>3</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>29</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>29</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>29</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>. If the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> ...(i)
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math> (angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> )
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> 
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> ...(ii)
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> (using (ii))
<math><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> (using (i))
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
Hence, the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> on <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>
