### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>If <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mi>y</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mtext>.</mtext> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>-</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mtext>.</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfenced> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>

                         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>-</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mtext>.</mtext> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>

Therefore, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>j</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>.</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mtext>j</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>.</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mtext>j</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>.</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mtext>i</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mtext>j</mtext><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>i</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mi>y</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>j</mtext> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mi>y</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mi>y</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mi>z</mi> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>a</mi></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>b</mi></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>c</mi></msub></math> are <math><mn>3</mn></math> unit vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>a</mi></msub><mo>⋅</mo><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>b</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>a</mi></msub><mo>⋅</mo><msub><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mi>c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the angle between <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#8901;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#8901;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub></math> is perpendicular to <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
Therefore, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> be a unit vector coplanar with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>. If the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **12**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>
Projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> on <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>12</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are non-parallel unit vectors and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is a vector which is perpendicular to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>21</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is parallel to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>&#215;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
Let <math><mi>&#952;</mi></math> be angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mtext>&#8201;</mtext></math>
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are non coplanar non - zero vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> then which of the following is not true

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> <math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mo>.</mo><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Similarly, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi></msqrt></math>

Now, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

But, <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>≠</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (non-coplanar)

So <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

Hence <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The projection of <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> on the line whose equation is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, where <math><mi>λ</mi></math> is a scalar parameter, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>41</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>41</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>41</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>32</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>41</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given line is parallel to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> (let)
Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> on <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math> <mrow>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <mover accent='true'>     <mi>a</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>     <mi>b</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="|" open="|">     <mover accent='true'>      <mi>b</mi>      <mo>&#x2192;</mo>     </mover>         </mfenced></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <msqrt>     <mrow>      <mn>41</mn></mrow>    </msqrt>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>32</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <msqrt>     <mrow>      <mn>41</mn></mrow>    </msqrt>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be a vector in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math>- plane making an angle of <math><msup><mrow><mn>6</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math> with the positive <math><mi>x</mi></math>-axis and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is the geometric mean of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Given, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt></math>
On simplifying, we get, </p><p> <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The vectors <math><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are mutually perpendicular and the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are also mutually perpendicular. Then the angle between the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>π</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>⋅</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>             ...(i)

and  <math><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>⋅</mo><mo>(</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>             ...(ii)

 <math><mo>∴</mo></math>     From equation (i) and (ii),

 <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>43</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>43</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mn>43</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>26</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>26</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>-</mo><mn>26</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
Taking dot product with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⇒</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>λ</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p><p>i.e. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
Taking dot product with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>29</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>26</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#305;</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>50</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#305;</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
on comparison
<math><mi>x</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
Squaring and adding equation <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>i</mi><mi>i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are three vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mn>0</mn><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mo>-</mo><mn>864</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1863**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>144</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>24</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>576</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>351</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Similarly, <math><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>576</mn><mo>+</mo><mi></mi><mn>36</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>144</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>513</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>36</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
And, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>36</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><msup><mo>|</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><msup><mo>|</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>576</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>144</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>81</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>213</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>864</mn><mo>(</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>12636</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>12312</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>11502</mn></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1863</mn></math>

</details>

mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math> and the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><msup><mn>30</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>|</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> &hellip; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Given, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If for a plane, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>z</mi></math> intercepts are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>4</mn></math> respectively, then the length of the perpendicular from the origin on to the plane is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> units
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math> units
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> units

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>8</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> units**

**Detailed Solution:**

Equation of plane is <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>⇒   </mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

Length of perpendicular from origin to <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are unit vectors such that <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2020</mn><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>133</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>133</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>30</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>120</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>133</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>8</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2020</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>28</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>21</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>28</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>21</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>=</mo><mn>29</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>17</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>133</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, where <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are non-collinear vectors ( <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is a unit vector) and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi></math> are scalars, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>11</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>13</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>13</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
Comparing both sides, we get, 
<math><mn>14</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>
