### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>. If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be a unit vector, then the magnitude of the vector <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> can be written as a linear combination of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
Similarly <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math>
Hence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>S</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>4</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A point <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#947;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> satisfies the equation of the plane <math><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> satisfies the relation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>3</mn><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> &hellip;. <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#947;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
From equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>, we get,
<math><mn>4</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.7</mn><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For three vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, if <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>|</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>[</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>]</mo></math>
Given <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi></mi><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
Taking dot product with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, we get, 
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> be an equilateral triangle with side length unity (<math><mi>O</mi></math> being the origin). Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math> are the points of trisection of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> being closer to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math> being closer to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math>. Position vectors of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>m</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> respectively, then the value of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/23456ujhbvfr56890plkjhgfdrt8.JPG" style="width: 184px; height: 165px;" />
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi></math> are any <math><mn>4</mn></math> points in space. If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is the area of triangle <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mi>λ</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, the position vectors are <math><mi>A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi>D</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
So, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If a line passing through <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is perpendicular to the vector <math><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and parallel to the plane containing vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>&#946;</mi><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then <math><mn>10</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>53</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn><mn>6</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>53</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, coordinates of <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi></math> are respectively <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4,1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> respectively
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>∵</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to <math><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Also, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> are coplanar
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>6</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi></mtd><mtd><mi>α</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>β</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>12</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><mn>12</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>α</mi></math>
<math><mn>12</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>28</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>12</mn><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are two vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **18**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>
and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Hence, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>324</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are three vectors, such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>12</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>12</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>144</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
As we know that,
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be three vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math> If the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is double of the projection of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are perpendicular to each other, then the value of <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>41</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>14</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>14</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>20.5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>20.5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p><p> <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

So, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>36</mn></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> be three vectors such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>15</mn></msqrt><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></math> is equal to (here, the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is acute)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>±</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>±</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>±</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mo>±</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mn>1</mn></mfenced></math>
Now, <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mn>15</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
The angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is acute
Hence, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
from <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are vectors perpendicular to each other and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then the least value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>cot</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8805;</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8805;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be two non-collinear vectors and a triangle <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> with side lengths <math><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>b</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>c</mi></math> satisfy the vector equation
 <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>15</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>12</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>,
then <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** an acute angle triangle
- **(B)** an obtuse angle triangle
- **(C)** a right angle triangle
- **(D)** an isosceles triangle.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) a right angle triangle**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> are non coplanar vectors
<math><mn>20</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>15</mn><mi>b</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>12</mn><mi>c</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is a right angle triangle

</details>

---

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>P</mi></math> and <math><mi>Q</mi></math> are two points in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math> plane on the curve <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mo>–</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></math> such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, then the magnitude of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>14</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>12</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>P</mi></math> and <math><mi>Q</mi></math> be <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
Let <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>11</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn></math>
&there4; <math><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>y</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>5</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></math>

### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mo>|</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>84</mn></msqrt></math> , then the value of <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> may be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>6</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> 

Let  <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> 

 <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi></math>     

 <math><mo>|</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>|</mo><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>84</mn></msqrt><mo>⇒</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>84</mn></msqrt></math> 

 <math><mi>z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>;</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> 

 <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> 

 <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>–</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Consider a parallelogram constructed on the vectors <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo></math> If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>p</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>q</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo></math> the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math> and the length of the smallest diagonal of the parallelogram is <math><mi>k</mi></math> units, then the value of <math><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **109**

**Detailed Solution:**

Diagonals of the parallelogram are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>A</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>36</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>144</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>109</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>16</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>40</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>16</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>25</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>40</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>889</mn></msqrt></math>
So, length of the smaller diagonal is <math><msqrt><mn>109</mn></msqrt></math> units

</details>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> be three unit vectors perpendicular to each other, then <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>18</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|"> <mrow> <mn>10</mn><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>18</mn><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mn>32</mn><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>as&#160;</mtext><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
