### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>j</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>,</mo></mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mo> </mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"> <mi>j</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>,</mo><mo> </mo></mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>c</mi> <mo>→</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>. If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced></math> is equal to
use <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>73</mn></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3.46**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
As, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
Now <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable columnalign="left"><mtr><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3.4</mn><mn>6</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are three non-collinear vectors in a plane such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>29</mn></msqrt><mo>.</mo></math> If the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and the angle between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> (property of triangles)
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3cde4290-1990-4fc6-a321-a30751e65b1b.png" style="width:1.58681in;height:1.36042in" />
From the diagram,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are three unit vectors, then sum of all possible values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>12</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>14</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>14</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
Taking dot with <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> also <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> (from given equation)
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> or <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
So, if <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>
or if <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mo> </mo><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>. If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>155</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>17</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msqrt><mn>155</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
Taking right cross product with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>, we get, 
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>155</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> be two unit vectors such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math> For some <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>R,</mi></math> let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and the vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is inclined at the same angle <math> <mi>α</mi> </math> to both <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>a</mi> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>b</mi> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> </mrow> <mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math> <mrow> <mn>8</mn><msup> <mrow> <mi>cos</mi></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>α</mi></mrow> </math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

Also, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi> <mi>&#945;</mi> </mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<math><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></mfrac><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mi>b</mi></mfrac><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>b</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>b</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>b</mi><mo>&#62;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the maximum value of <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mo>∵</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>≥</mo><mn>4</mn></math>  
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>≥</mo><mn>5</mn></math>  
<math><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>≥</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>≥</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>β</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfrac><mo>≤</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>Two points <math><mi>P</mi></math> and <math><mi>Q</mi></math> are lying on the curve <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> <em>in</em> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mi>y</mi></math> plane such that <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is</p><p>(where, <math><mi>O</mi></math> is the origin)</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><msqrt><mn>8</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Q</mi></math> <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>P</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1,2</mn><mo>)</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>Q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>5,3</mn></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

In a <math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math>, the sides <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>C</mi><mi>A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></math> are consecutive positive integers in increasing order. Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> are position vectors of the vertices <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> respectively. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#183;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>,<mn></mn> then the value of <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **12**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mover accent='true'>     <mi>c</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mover accent='true'>     <mi>a</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    </mrow>  </mfenced><mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mfenced>   <mrow>    <mover accent='true'>     <mi>b</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mover accent='true'>     <mi>c</mi>     <mo>&#x2192;</mo>    </mover>    </mrow>  </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#x21D2;</mo><mover accent='true'>   <mrow>    <mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow>   <mo stretchy='true'>&#x2192;</mo>  </mover>  <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>   <mrow>    <mi>C</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow>   <mo stretchy='true'>&#x2192;</mo>  </mover>  <mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#x21D2;</mo><mi>&#x0394;</mi><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></math> is right angle at <math><mi>C</mi><mo>.</mo></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/889dbe61-7e42-4e24-904d-446b00f78aeb.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.86667in;height:1.31319in" />
From diagram
<math><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>k</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>⇒</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∴</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>≠</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
So, sides are <math><mn>3</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>4</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn></math>
Area of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn></math>

</details>

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mi>O</mi></math> be an interior point of triangle <math><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C,</mi></math> such that <math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> then the ratio of the area of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></math> to the area of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>3</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/8be6d8cb-604a-42d2-bd7f-ac8bd9b67df1.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.11319in;height:1.54028in" />
Let position vectors of <math><mi>O</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>C</mi></math> are <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> respectively.
<math><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Area of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
Area of <math><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>A</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>|</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>|</mo></math>
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mo>∆</mo><mi>A</mi><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If two points <math><mi>P</mi></math> and <math><mi>Q</mi></math> are on the curve <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> such that <math> <mover accent="true"> <mrow> <mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </math> and <math> <mover accent="true"> <mrow> <mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo> </math> where <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is a unit vector along the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis, then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **10**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let <math><mi>P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mi>Q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> be two points on <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math> <mo>∵</mo><mover accent="true"> <mrow> <mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo><msub> <mi>x</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn> </math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math> <mover accent="true"> <mrow> <mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow> <mo stretchy="true">→</mo> </mover> <mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>i</mi> <mo>^</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>⇒</mo><msub> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn> </math> and <math><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi></mi></math> and <math><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>Q</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>O</mi><mi>P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

</details>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>U</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>W</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>.</mo></math> If <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is a unit vector such that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>U</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and  <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>W</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8901;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵ </mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is perpendicular to both <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
Hence, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
Since <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is unit vector hence <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Hence, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
So, <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>W</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is equally inclined with the vectors <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> then the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>cos</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are unit vectors and mutually perpendicular vectors and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is equally inclined to all <math><mn>3</mn></math> vectors
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>λ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
Angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>λ</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>λ</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q72. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and non-zero vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> are such that <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>, then vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> may be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math> <mfenced>  <mrow>   <mover accent='true'>    <mi>a</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>    <mi>c</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   </mrow> </mfenced><mover accent='true'>  <mi>b</mi>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo> </mover> <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfenced>  <mrow>   <mover accent='true'>    <mi>b</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>    <mi>c</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   </mrow> </mfenced><mover accent='true'>  <mi>a</mi>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo> </mover> <mo>=</mo><mfenced>  <mrow>   <mover accent='true'>    <mi>a</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>    <mi>c</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   </mrow> </mfenced><mover accent='true'>  <mi>b</mi>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo> </mover> <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfenced>  <mrow>   <mover accent='true'>    <mi>a</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   <mo>&#x22C5;</mo><mover accent='true'>    <mi>b</mi>    <mo>&#x2192;</mo>   </mover>   </mrow> </mfenced><mover accent='true'>  <mi>c</mi>  <mo>&#x2192;</mo> </mover> </math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> must be parallel to <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math>

</details>

### Q73. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi>C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>D</mi></math> are <math><mn>4</mn></math> points in space satisfying the equation <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let position vectors of <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>D</mi></math> are <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> respectively
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>⋅</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>C</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mrow><mi>B</mi><mi>D</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>d</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>d</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>a</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><mi>d</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi>b</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover><mi>d</mi><mo>→</mo></mover></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>⋅</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> … <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
From <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> &amp; <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>,
<math><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q74. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let the volume of a parallelepiped whose coterminous edges are given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>u</mi><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>λ</mi><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover><mrow><mo> </mo><mi>v</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> cu. unit. If <math><mi>θ</mi></math> be the angle between the edges <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>u</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>w</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> then the value of <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mi>λ</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>3</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>2</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo> </mo></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>±</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo> </mo><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>  
For <math><mi>λ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt><msqrt><mn>18</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q75. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sec</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>s</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, where <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> are unit vectors and the angle between <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>b</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is <math><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math>, then the minimum value of <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>
